# The Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) Regulations 2001

> Text as at 2021-11-26.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, NI.

*Extent: E+W+S+NI*

2001 No. 3510

AGRICULTURE

The Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) Regulations 2001 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

Made *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

29th October 2001 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

Laid before Parliament *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

31st October 2001 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

Coming into force *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

1st December 2001 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

The Secretary of State for Environment, Food and Rural Affairs, the Secretary of State for Scotland and the National Assembly for Wales, being designated *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00001] for the purposes of section 2(2) of the European Communities Act 1972 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00002] in relation to the common agricultural policy of the European Community, acting jointly (the National Assembly for Wales acting in relation to Wales only) in exercise of the powers conferred on them by that section, hereby make the following Regulations:— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

## 1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Title and commencement (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

These Regulations may be cited as the Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) Regulations 2001 and shall come into force on 1st December 2001. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

## 2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] In these Regulations, unless the context otherwise requires— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- “the[^key-1eed905a4f85173b24bf6ce2e26da11d] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] 1990 Directive *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]” means Council Directive *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [90/220/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EEC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1990/0220)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00004] on the deliberate release into the environment of genetically modified organisms, as amended by Commission Directive *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [94/15/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1994/0015)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00005] and Commission Directive *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [97/35/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1997/0035)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00006]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the appropriate authority” means—[^key-f6d99f63b347b26a3426e8c4f9365f83] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (a) in relation to England, the Secretary of State; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (b) in relation to Scotland, the Scottish Ministers; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (c) in relation to Wales, the Welsh Ministers; and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (d) in relation to the acceptance of a plant variety onto a GB Variety List, the Secretary of State, the Welsh Ministers and the Scottish Ministers, acting jointly; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “authorised officer” means an officer authorised for the purposes of these Regulations by the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Secretary of State *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605], the National Assembly for Wales *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] or the Scottish Ministers[^key-1fac9762ce09215b4539e6c72eb2a3c4] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “a B List vegetable variety” means a variety whose seed may not be controlled except as standard seed; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “certified”, in relation to seed, has the same meaning as in the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Seeds Marketing Regulations *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] and “certification” shall be construed accordingly; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- [^key-c214f2f62a06e48730c61e268966bcf5]... *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- [^key-f7573c79d421610c420658e2d9b86a16]... *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the Common Catalogue Directive” means Council Directive[^key-1c2d5b60c8f12f02330f81275b2ff1b9] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [2002/53/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2002/0053)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00007] on the common catalogue of varieties of agricultural plant species, as amended by the Food and Feed Regulation; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “conservation variety” means a landrace or plant variety of a species specified in Part I of Schedule 1 that is naturally adapted to the local and regional conditions (or, in the case of a vegetable variety, that has been traditionally grown in particular localities and regions) and is threatened by genetic erosion, and for these purposes—[^key-a45a943d62b3528ddc590f978e145162] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (a) “landrace” means a set of populations or clones of a plant species which are naturally adapted to the environmental conditions of their region; and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (b) “genetic erosion” means loss of genetic diversity between and within populations or varieties of the same species over time, or reduction of the genetic basis of a species due to human intervention or environmental change; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the Deliberate Release Directive” means Council Directive[^key-76953d0b22fb2a73ebdb0d82f9d93afd] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [2001/18/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2001/0018)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00008] on the deliberate release into the environment of genetically modified organisms, as *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] last amended by Directive (EU) 2015/412 of the European Parliament and of the Council[^key-f4527cbe32ef0c4e29a8960e8e323035] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “Department of Agriculture and Rural Development” means the Department of Agriculture and Rural Development in Northern Ireland; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “derogated species” means a species of plant specified in Part II of Schedule 1; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the Equivalence Decision” means Council Decision[^key-74b310001273f9bf4ec2c3e44024311a] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [2005/834/ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/decision/2005/0834)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] on the equivalence of checks on practices for the maintenance of varieties carried out in certain third countries *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] , as last amended by Council Regulation (EU) 517/2013[^key-b3948271242f3952c2c20c3bc6924b03] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] fees Regulations *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]” means the Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) (Fees) Regulations 1994 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00005]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the Food and Feed Regulation” means Council Regulation[^key-305533e8a9c36eac1c84b9298270eaa3] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [( *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EC *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*) No 1829/2003 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/2003/1829)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00016] on genetically modified food and feed *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] , as last amended by Regulation[^key-27bb64264146513f5fcbefda2adecdf5] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [(EC) No 298/2008 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/2008/0298)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] of the European Parliament and of the Council *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “ *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]the Gazette *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]” means the Plant Varieties and Seeds Gazette published in accordance with section 34(1) of the Plant Varieties and Seeds Act 1964 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^f00006]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the GB Variety Lists” means the lists of varieties of species of agricultural plants and varieties of species of vegetables, prepared and published in accordance with regulation 3, and “GB Variety List” means any one of those lists;[^key-9b6e806da212a289a3ac1d9cbf99ddae] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “genetically modified” has the same meaning as for the purposes of the Deliberate Release Directive;[^key-eeae2861a9e0d5491477440c945c305a] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “maintainer”, in respect of a plant variety, means a person named in or ascertainable by reference to a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List[^key-15506d4f9f5449982123b303702b73e7] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] as responsible for maintaining a variety in accordance with the characteristics to which regard was had when that variety was accepted on a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; and “maintenance” shall be construed accordingly; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “name”, in respect of a plant variety, includes denomination; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- [^key-f7573c79d421610c420658e2d9b86a16]... *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- [^key-f7573c79d421610c420658e2d9b86a16]... *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the NI Variety Lists” means the lists of varieties of species of agricultural plants and varieties of species of vegetable, prepared and published by the Department of Agriculture and Rural Development in accordance with legislation having effect corresponding to that of regulation 3(1)(a) and (2) to (5)[^key-e7b5c844cdbd30e1c834299c9888b8ce] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] , and “[^key-f9a60d1d8971aa00a194a5511a9b7479] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]**NI Variety List *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]**” means any one of those lists *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “[^key-d3ac6158ff350efd7ae42d13ed95eb47] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]**the Novel Foods Regulation *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]**” means Council Regulation *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] [(EC) No 258/97 *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eur/1997/258)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] concerning novel foods and novel food ingredients, as it applied immediately before 18 April 2004; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “official measures” includes official examinations, growing trials, tests and assessments; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “person affected”, in respect of a proposed relevant decision or a relevant decision, means— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (a) any person to whom the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] appropriate authority[^key-dee7627020f84654c0af3387f7e74aa9] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] have given notice of the proposed relevant decision or the relevant decision; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (b) any person appearing to the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] appropriate authority *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] to have a sufficient interest in the matter to which the decision relates; and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (c) any person or organisation appearing to them to be representative of a class of persons likely to be affected by the decision; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “region of origin” means the region identified by the[^key-d37a52ceb5173eb75bc1d048149c6cb1] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] appropriate authority *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] when a conservation variety is accepted on to a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] as being the region in which the variety has historically been grown and to which it is naturally developed; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “relevant decision” means a decision of the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] appropriate authority *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (a) under regulation 5 to accept or refuse to accept a plant variety on to a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (b) that they are satisfied a plant variety has a name suitable, in accordance with regulation 6, for publication in *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] the Gazette *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (c) to accept, or refuse to accept, an application under regulation 7; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (d) to renew or not to renew the acceptance of a variety under regulation 13; or *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
  - (e) to revoke the acceptance of a variety under regulation 14, replace the original acceptance of a variety under that regulation or delete the name of a person as the maintainer of a variety under regulation 14(4)(e); *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- “the relevant legislation” means—[^key-f4b0efe4bc3da44895ad18798d30cd69] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] any person who was purporting to act in any such capacity, he, as well as the body corporate, shall be guilty of the offence and shall be liable to be proceeded against and punished accordingly. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(3 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] For the purposes of paragraph (1) above, “director”, in relation to a body corporate whose affairs are managed by its members, means a member of the body corporate. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(4 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where an offence under these Regulations which has been committed by a Scottish partnership is proved to have been committed with the consent or connivance of, or to be attributable to any neglect on the part of, a partner, he as well as the partnership shall be guilty of the offence and shall be liable to be proceeded against and punished accordingly. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

## 20 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Offences due to fault of another person and defence of due diligence (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where the commission by any person of an offence under any of the provisions of these Regulations is due to the act or default of some other person, that other person shall be guilty of the offence; and a person may be charged with and convicted of the offence by virtue of this paragraph of this regulation whether or not proceedings are taken against the first-mentioned person. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] In any proceedings for an offence under any of the provisions of these Regulations, it shall, subject to paragraph (3) below, be a defence for the person charged to prove that he took all reasonable precautions and exercised all due diligence to avoid the commission of the offence by himself or by a person under his control. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(3 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] If in any case the defence provided by paragraph (2) above involves the allegation that the commission of the offence was due to an act or default of another person, or to reliance on information supplied by another person, the person charged shall not, without leave of the court, be entitled to rely on that defence unless— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- (a (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] at least seven clear days before the hearing; and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- (b (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] where he has previously appeared before a court in connection with the alleged offence, within one month of his first such appearance, *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

he has served on the prosecutor a notice in writing giving such information identifying or assisting in the identification of that other person as was then in his possession. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(4 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] In paragraph (3) above any reference to appearing before a court shall be construed as including a reference to being brought before a court. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

## 21 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Application of provisions of the Plant Varieties and Seeds Act 1964 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] As respects Great Britain, the following provisions of the Plant Varieties and Seeds Act 1964 shall apply for the purposes of these Regulations as they apply for the purposes of seeds Regulations having effect under Part II of that Act and, unless the context otherwise requires, any reference in them to seeds Regulations or to that Part shall be construed as a reference to these Regulations and any reference to a person duly authorised by the Minister or to an authorised officer shall be taken to be references to an authorised officer within the meaning of these Regulations— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- section 25 (powers of entry); *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- section 26 (use of samples in criminal proceedings); *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- section 27 (tampering with samples); *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- section 28 (institution of criminal proceedings), subject to the modification that it shall apply to proceedings in respect of any offence under these Regulations as it applies to the proceedings to which it refers; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- section 29(1) (application of Part II to seed potatoes); and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- section 30(2) (interpretation of Part II: contravention and failure of compliance). *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5420aa720a4fab83c8a8bbec339d4981][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

## SCHEDULE 2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) — DISTINCTNESS, STABILITY, SUFFICIENT UNIFORMITY AND SATISFACTORY VALUE FOR CULTIVATION AND USE (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

Regulation 5(3)(b) and (c) *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

### Part I (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) — Distinctness, stability and sufficient uniformity (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Distinctness (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] A plant variety shall be regarded as distinct if, whatever the origin, artificial or natural, of the initial variation from which it has resulted, it is clearly distinguishable in one or more important characteristics from any other variety known in the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] United Kingdom or accepted onto an official register of plant varieties corresponding to a GB Variety List in a country outside the United Kingdom with similar growing conditions[^key-4936143efd6b2af64e18d2fd733dfa48] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] at the time when the application for acceptance of the variety is duly made. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] The characteristics of a variety must be capable of precise recognition and precise definition. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(3 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-ff972ea70a584f9f87ba3a4c464e1878][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where, at the time when an application for acceptance of a variety is duly made, a variety is known in the United Kingdom (or accepted onto an official register of plant varieties corresponding to a GB Variety List in a country outside the United Kingdom with similar growing conditions), but when that application is determined the variety is no longer known in the United Kingdom (or accepted onto that official register), that variety must be disregarded for the purpose of determining distinctness as part of that application. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 1A (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note).[^M_F_e9123eb0-b4ac-4756-bd57-a3dd9b1a092f][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] A plant variety of a conservation variety is regarded as distinct if it satisfies the characteristics referred to in— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- (a (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] the technical questionnaires associated with the test protocols listed in Annex I to Directive 2003/90 or Directive 2003/91 applicable to the species in question, or *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- (b (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] the technical questionnaires of the test guidelines listed in Annex II to those Directives, applicable to the species in question. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] In this paragraph— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- (a (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] “Directive 2003/90” means Commission Directive 2003/90/EC setting out implementing measures for the purposes of Article 7 of Council Directive 2002/53/EC as regards the characteristics to be covered as a minimum by the examination and the minimum conditions for examining certain varieties of agricultural plant species, as amended from time to time, and *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- (b (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] “Directive 2003/91” means Commission Directive 2003/91/EC setting out implementing measures for the purposes of Article 7 of Council Directive 2002/55/EC as regards the characteristics to be covered as a minimum by the examination and the minimum conditions for examining certain varieties of vegetable species, as amended from time to time. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Stability (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

A variety shall be regarded as stable if, after successive propagation or multiplications, or, where the applicant has defined a particular cycle of propagations or multiplications, at the end of each cycle, it remains true to the description of its essential characteristics. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 3 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Sufficient uniformity (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

A plant variety shall be regarded as sufficiently uniform if, apart from a very few aberrations, the plants of which it is composed are, account being taken of the distinctive features of the reproductive system of the plants, similar or genetically identical as regards the characteristics, taken as a whole, considered by the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] appropriate authority[^key-6727f021ce9d9d79c78f0a5fee509b83] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] for the purpose of determining whether the variety is sufficiently uniform. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

### Part II (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) — Satisfactory value for cultivation and use (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Satisfactory value for cultivation and use (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] The value of a variety for cultivation or use shall be regarded as satisfactory if compared to other varieties accepted in a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List[^key-e15af0b6c59a43a112bded077f6d1797] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] its qualities taken as a whole offer, at least as far as production in any given region is concerned, a clear improvement either for cultivation or as regards the uses which can be made of the crops or the products derived from the crops. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where other superior characteristics are present individual inferior characteristics may be disregarded. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

#### 2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note). Satisfactory value for cultivation and use of component varieties of varietal associations (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(1 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where a variety is intended solely for use as a component of a varietal association the value of the variety for cultivation or use shall be regarded as satisfactory if compared to other varieties accepted in a *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] GB Variety List[^key-039bdeb8d46dcec3d8f0373360b7edce] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] the qualities of the varietal association taken as a whole offer, at least as far as production in any given region is concerned, a clear improvement either for cultivation or as regards the uses which can be made of the crops or the products derived from the crops resulting from the varietal association. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(2 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] Where other superior characteristics are present in the varietal association individual inferior characteristics in it may be disregarded. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

### Part III (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) — Varieties (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) the appropriate authority (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-3c089afcf592c45d49aa25c92c832c81][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

Part II of this Schedule does not apply to— *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

- (b (Extent: N.I.) - (c (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-e6dc7735f26392ab3ff8fc1f7112efa2][^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- (d (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] any variety intended for use only as a component of a final plant variety; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] or[^M_F_338dd327-9dfd-4471-fee6-0040423a1b51] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]
- (e (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605] any conservation variety *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bc6d6b63388356e8d490897aeeb36605]

(This note is not part of the Regulations)

These Regulations, which extend to the whole of the United Kingdom, revoke and replace the Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) Regulations 1982, S.I. [1982/844](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/1982/844) as amended. The Regulations give effect to Council Directives [70/457/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1970/0457) (OJ No. L225, 12.10.1970, p. 1) on the common catalogue of varieties of agricultural plant species and those elements of Council Directive [70/458/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1970/0458) (OJ No. L225, 12.10.1970, p. 7) on the marketing of vegetable seed which require member States to establish national lists of vegetable varieties. These directives were last amended by Council Directive [98/95/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1998/0095) (OJ No. L25, 1.2.1999, p. 1) in respect of the consolidation of the internal market, genetically modified plant varieties and plant genetic resources and Council Directive [98/96/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1998/0096) (OJ No. L25, 1.2.1999, p. 27) as regards naming of plant varieties. The amending directives also amended directives in respect of the marketing of seeds.

Certain requirements of Council Directive [98/95/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1998/0095) have not been transposed in full in these Regulations where there is existing provision in the Environmental Protection Act [1990 (c. 43)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1990/43). Part VI of that Act makes general provision for preventing damage to the environment from the release of genetically modified organisms. Relevant provisions include carrying out environmental risk assessments for genetically modified plant varieties, imposition of conditions appropriate for their cultivation and prohibition of their use where their cultivation could be harmful.

Regulation 1 provides the title of the Regulations and the coming into force date of 1st December 2001. Regulation 2 defines expressions used in the Regulations and provides that expressions in the Regulations not so defined and which appear in Council Directives [70/457/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1970/0457) and [70/458/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1970/0458) have the same meaning in the Regulations as they have in those directives. Regulation 3 makes provision in relation to National Lists of plant varieties prepared and published in the Plant Varieties and Seeds Gazette.

Regulation 4 provides for applications in relation to national lists, regulation 5 provides for acceptance of plant varieties and regulation 6 makes provision for the naming of plant varieties. The National Authorities must be satisfied of the matters referred to in regulation 5(3) for acceptance on to National Lists of plant varieties of the species specified in Schedule 1. These include matters relating to genetically modified organisms; distinctness, stability and sufficient uniformity described in Part I of Schedule 2; and, in respect of the species of plant varieties other than the species described in Part III of Schedule 2, value for cultivation and use as described in Part II of Schedule 2.

Regulation 7 makes provision in relation to maintainers of plant varieties. Regulation 8 makes provision in relation to official measures and regulation 9 makes provision for arrangements for official measures. Regulation 10 makes provision for the charging of fees. National Authorities and persons with whom arrangements are made under regulation 9 may charge reasonable fees to cover the costs they reasonably incur in carrying out official measures. National Authorities may also charge persons concerned in matters connected with the Regulations reasonable fees to cover the costs the National Authorities reasonably incur in relation to the administration of official measures for the purposes of these Regulations; for example the administration and determination of applications for acceptance of plant varieties on to the National Lists.

Regulation 11 makes provision for marketing prohibition notices. Regulation 12 makes provision for maintenance of plant varieties accepted on to national lists. Regulation 13 provides that acceptance of plant varieties is initially for a period of 10 years and provides the conditions for renewal of acceptance. Regulation 14 provides for revocation of acceptance of plant varieties and maintainers of plant varieties and regulation 15 makes provision for records. Regulation 16 makes provision for representations and hearings in respect of proposed relevant decisions and regulation 17 makes provision for appeals against relevant decisions, except those in respect of naming of plant varieties.

Regulation 18 makes provision for obstruction and regulation 19 for offences and penalties. Regulation 20 makes provision for offences due to the fault of another person and the defence of due diligence, regulation 21 applies provisions of the Plant Varieties and Seeds Act 1964 and the Seeds Act (Northern Ireland) 1965 for the purposes of the Regulations, regulation 22 makes provision for enforcement and regulation 23 for service of notices etc. Regulation 24 revokes the Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) Regulations 1982, and the provisions of the Seeds (National Lists of Varieties) (Fees) Regulations 1994 replaced by regulation 10 of these Regulations, with consequential amendments, savings and transitional provisions. Regulation 25 amends Seeds Marketing Regulations.

A regulatory impact assessment has been prepared for these Regulations and placed in the library of each House of Parliament. Copies of the assessment can be obtained from the Plant Variety Rights Office and Seeds Division of the Department for Environment, Food and Rural Affairs, White House Lane, Huntingdon Road, Cambridge CB3 0LF.
